<style id="apc-inline-inline-css">


    .apc-card{
      display:flex; gap:28px;
 
    }
    .apc-left{flex:0 0 auto}
    .apc-photo-wrap{background:#fff;width:120px;height:120px;border-radius:999px;overflow:hidden;box-shadow:0 10px 25px rgba(0,0,0,.25)}
    .apc-photo{width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;display:block}
    .apc-right{min-width:0}
    .apc-name{margin:0 0 10px;font-size:38px!important;line-height:1.05;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:-.02em}
	.apc-after-title{
	display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 20px;
	}
    .apc-title{margin:0 0 0px;font-size:18px!important;font-weight:600!important;}
    .apc-social{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;margin:0px 0 8px}
    .apc-icon{
      width:38px;height:38px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
      border-radius:999px;background:#009EE0;
      border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.18);
      transition:transform .15s ease, background .15s ease;
    }
    .apc-icon:hover{transform:translateY(-1px);background:#004C7A}
    .apc-icon svg{width:18px;height:18px;fill:#fff;opacity:.95}
    .apc-bio{font-size:16px;line-height:1.5;}
    @media(max-width:768px){
      .apc-card{flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start}
      .apc-photo-wrap{width:160px;height:160px}
      .apc-name{font-size:34px}
    }
/*# sourceURL=apc-inline-inline-css */
</style>

<style type="text/css">.blue-message {
background: none repeat scroll 0 0 #3399ff;
    color: #ffffff;
    text-shadow: none;
    font-size: 16px;
    line-height: 24px;
    padding: 10px;
}.green-message {
background: none repeat scroll 0 0 #8cc14c;
    color: #ffffff;
    text-shadow: none;
    font-size: 16px;
    line-height: 24px;
    padding: 10px;
}.orange-message {
background: none repeat scroll 0 0 #faa732;
    color: #ffffff;
    text-shadow: none;
    font-size: 16px;
    line-height: 24px;
    padding: 10px;
}.red-message {
background: none repeat scroll 0 0 #da4d31;
    color: #ffffff;
    text-shadow: none;
    font-size: 16px;
    line-height: 24px;
    padding: 10px;
}.grey-message {
background: none repeat scroll 0 0 #53555c;
    color: #ffffff;
    text-shadow: none;
    font-size: 16px;
    line-height: 24px;
    padding: 10px;
}.left-block {
background: none repeat scroll 0 0px, radial-gradient(ellipse at center center, #ffffff 0%, #f2f2f2 100%) repeat scroll 0 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0);
    color: #8b8e97;
    padding: 10px;
    margin: 10px;
    float: left;
}.right-block {
background: none repeat scroll 0 0px, radial-gradient(ellipse at center center, #ffffff 0%, #f2f2f2 100%) repeat scroll 0 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0);
    color: #8b8e97;
    padding: 10px;
    margin: 10px;
    float: right;
}.blockquotes {
background: none;
    border-left: 5px solid #f1f1f1;
    color: #8B8E97;
    font-size: 16px;
    font-style: italic;
    line-height: 22px;
    padding-left: 15px;
    padding: 10px;
    width: 60%;
    float: left;

<section class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-row alignfull gspb_row gspb_row-id-gsbp-3f3c1b3" id="gspb_row-id-gsbp-3f3c1b3"><div class="gspb_row__content"> 
<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-row-column gspb_row__col--12 gspb_col-id-gsbp-848af24" id="gspb_col-id-gsbp-848af24">
<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-container gspb_container gspb_container-gsbp-4072c38" id="gspb_container-id-gsbp-4072c38">
<h2 id="gspb_heading-id-gsbp-356a899" class="gspb_heading gspb_heading-id-gsbp-356a899 ">Augmenting humans with AI and Robotics to accelerate Science</h2>
</div>



<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-row gspb_row gspb_row-id-gsbp-8dfebd3 gspb_row--gutter-custom-0" id="gspb_row-id-gsbp-8dfebd3"><div class="gspb_row__content"> 
<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-row-column gspb_row__col--6 gspb_col-id-gsbp-09440c2" id="gspb_col-id-gsbp-09440c2">
<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-image gspb_image gspb_image-id-gsbp-96b6c34" id="gspb_image-id-gsbp-96b6c34"><img decoding="async" src="https://atinary.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/SDLabs-AI-platform-laptop-scaled.webp" data-src="" alt="" loading="lazy" width="100%" height="370"/></div>



<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-container gspb_container gspb_container-gsbp-cf7cce9" id="gspb_container-id-gsbp-cf7cce9">
<h3 id="gspb_heading-id-gsbp-86b14c9" class="gspb_heading gspb_heading-id-gsbp-86b14c9 ">AI platform SDLabs</h3>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-db51ac4" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-db51ac4 ">Atinary’s AI-driven R&amp;D platform integrates machine learning (ML) optimizers, data analytics, and visualization into a single intuitive interface, enabling scientists to solve complex, multi-objective optimization challenges faster when designing and running experiments.</div>



<div class="gspb_button_wrapper gspb_button-id-gsbp-758b51d" id="gspb_button-id-gsbp-758b51d"><a class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-buttonbox gspb-buttonbox wp-element-button" href="https://atinary.com/technology/sdlabs/" rel="noopener"><span class="gspb-buttonbox-textwrap"><span class="gspb-buttonbox-text"><span class="gspb-buttonbox-title">Meet SDLabs</span></span></span></a></div>
</div>
</div>



<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-row-column gspb_row__col--6 gspb_col-id-gsbp-5ba6ba7" id="gspb_col-id-gsbp-5ba6ba7">
<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-image gspb_image gspb_image-id-gsbp-848825e" id="gspb_image-id-gsbp-848825e"><img decoding="async" src="https://atinary.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Automated_lab_robotic_arm-1.webp" data-src="" alt="Enclosed laboratory automation cell with a robotic arm manipulating equipment and racks of red-capped vials. Sensors, grippers, and conveyors line the workstation inside a glass enclosure in a modern industrial research facility. This is showcasing Atinary's Self Driving Labs in Boston." loading="lazy" width="100%" height="370"/></div>



<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-container gspb_container gspb_container-gsbp-1faa468" id="gspb_container-id-gsbp-1faa468">
<h3 id="gspb_heading-id-gsbp-bb153e8" class="gspb_heading gspb_heading-id-gsbp-bb153e8 ">Self-Driving Labs®</h3>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-ab762b2" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-ab762b2 ">Atinary recently launched its own Self-Driving Lab, a smart data and IP factory in Boston, Massachusetts. With Self-Driving Labs® reimagine the physical laboratory by integrating AI, robotics, and human expertise into a unified, closed-loop ecosystem for faster innovation.</div>



<div class="gspb_button_wrapper gspb_button-id-gsbp-ecd1680" id="gspb_button-id-gsbp-ecd1680"><a class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-buttonbox gspb-buttonbox wp-element-button" href="https://atinary.com/technology/self-driving-labs/" rel="noopener"><span class="gspb-buttonbox-textwrap"><span class="gspb-buttonbox-text"><span class="gspb-buttonbox-title">Look into our Self-Driving Labs</span></span></span></a></div>
</div>
</div>
 </div></div>



<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-container gspb_container gspb_container-gsbp-94e1331" id="gspb_container-id-gsbp-94e1331">
<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-87a6f27" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-87a6f27 ">Learn more about our</div>



<div class="wp-block-group is-content-justification-center is-nowrap is-layout-flex wp-container-core-group-is-layout-82936891 wp-block-group-is-layout-flex">
<div class="gspb_button_wrapper gspb_button-id-gsbp-d69c57a" id="gspb_button-id-gsbp-d69c57a"><a class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-buttonbox gspb-buttonbox wp-element-button" href="/technology" title="Atinary Technology" rel="noopener"><span class="gspb-buttonbox-textwrap"><span class="gspb-buttonbox-text"><span class="gspb-buttonbox-title">Technology</span></span></span></a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
 </div></section>



<section class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-row alignfull gspb_row gspb_row-id-gsbp-1c7cd94" id="gspb_row-id-gsbp-1c7cd94"><div class="gspb_row__content"> 
<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-row-column gspb_row__col--12 gspb_col-id-gsbp-24cdc87" id="gspb_col-id-gsbp-24cdc87">
<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-container gspb_container gspb_container-gsbp-b90e6f4" id="gspb_container-id-gsbp-b90e6f4">
<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-c518bf5" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-c518bf5 ">Our mission is to build intelligent solutions for a healthier and more sustainable future.</div>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-072a367" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-072a367 ">By integrating AI and robotics with human expertise – our Self-Driving Labs® – we accelerate the development of molecules and materials among millions of possibilities.<br><br>We are reimagining the laboratory to dissolve traditional bottlenecks and unlock limitless science, enabling researchers to focus their expertise where it matters most: pure innovation.<br><br>Our technologies transform science and drive innovation in life-, chemical-, and materials sciences.</div>
</div>
</div>
 </div></section>



<section class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-row alignfull gspb_row gspb_row-id-gsbp-6462767" id="gspb_row-id-gsbp-6462767"><div class="gspb_row__content"> 
<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-row-column gspb_row__col--12 gspb_col-id-gsbp-709fba3" id="gspb_col-id-gsbp-709fba3">
<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-image gspb_image gspb_image-id-gsbp-19961a0" id="gspb_image-id-gsbp-19961a0"><img decoding="async" src="https://atinary.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/Atinary-SDLabs-Platform-closed-loop-detailed.webp" data-src="" alt="Illustration of Atinary's SDLabs Platform, showcasing their closed loop automation process in detail." loading="lazy" width="1330" height="1080"/></div>
</div>
 </div></section>



<section class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-row alignfull gspb_row gspb_row-id-gsbp-efef737 why_atinary" id="gspb_row-id-gsbp-efef737"><div class="gspb_row__content">

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-075ab5d" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-075ab5d ">The Leading Self-Driving Labs® Platform</div>
</div>



<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-container gspb_container gspb_container-gsbp-cf00183" id="gspb_container-id-gsbp-cf00183">

<div class="gs_content_area">
<div aria-labelledby="gs-trigger-gsbp-46b2433-0" id="gs-content-gsbp-46b2433-0" class="gs_content active" role="tabpanel" aria-hidden="false">
<div class="gs_content_inner">
<div class="gsbp-784203c">
<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-44b9ae2" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-44b9ae2 ">Thoughts Leaders and Pioneers of Self-Driving Labs®</div>



<ul style="margin-right:0;margin-left:0" class="wp-block-list">
<li>Founders coined the term <strong>Self-Driving Labs®</strong> and have been thought leaders in AI-driven R&amp;D since 2017.</li>



<li>Top multi-disciplinary team, including world-leading experts on its Scientific Advisory Board such as MIT <strong>Prof. Steve Buchwald</strong> (most cited chemist in the world between 1999 &#8211; 2009).</li>



<li class="advgb-dyn-3949af6c">Global partnerships and strong collaborations with leading companies, including <strong>ABB robotics</strong>, <strong>Agilent</strong>, <strong>AWS</strong>, <strong>Chemspeed</strong>, and <strong>Mettler Toledo</strong>, among others.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>



<div aria-labelledby="gs-trigger-gsbp-46b2433-1" id="gs-content-gsbp-46b2433-1" class="gs_content" role="tabpanel" aria-hidden="true">
<div class="gs_content_inner">
<div class="gsbp-39d7ff3">
<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-489841e" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-489841e ">Higher efficiency and productivity strengthens pipeline and competitiveness</div>



<ul style="margin-right:0;margin-left:0" class="wp-block-list">
<li>Efficient screening of chemical space to find <strong>global optima</strong> much faster.</li>



<li>Solve <strong>multi-parameter, multi-constraint, and multi-objective</strong> optimizations, faster, better, and at a fraction of the cost, today.</li>



<li><strong>Reduce development time and costs</strong> 5x to 100x compared to DOE and OFAT.</li>



<li><strong>Transition to digital R&amp;D</strong> for knowledge sharing and retention across your organization.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>



<div aria-labelledby="gs-trigger-gsbp-46b2433-2" id="gs-content-gsbp-46b2433-2" class="gs_content" role="tabpanel" aria-hidden="true">
<div class="gs_content_inner">
<div class="gsbp-b0410e9">
<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-19fa975" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-19fa975 ">Deploys Agentic Platform in Under Two Hours</div>



<ul style="margin-right:0;margin-left:0" class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Superior UI and UX, code-free agentic platform</strong> does not require any experience or knowledge in coding, machine learning, or data science to use.</li>



<li>Integrates into your <strong>existing workflows</strong> and get running in <strong>less than two hours.</strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>



<div aria-labelledby="gs-trigger-gsbp-46b2433-3" id="gs-content-gsbp-46b2433-3" class="gs_content" role="tabpanel" aria-hidden="true">

<div class="gsbp-cf7b170">
<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-e7411a9" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-e7411a9 ">Seamless Integration with Robotic Platforms (optional)</div>



<ul style="margin-right:0;margin-left:0" class="wp-block-list">
<li>Integrates with robotic hardware to enable <strong>Self-Driving Labs®</strong> mode.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-94e7af1" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-94e7af1 ">Expert Support and Enterprise-Grade Security</div>



<ul style="margin-right:0;margin-left:0" class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Superior customer support</strong> from our multi-disciplinary team of scientists &amp; engineers</li>



<li><strong>State-of-the-art </strong>data, privacy, and IP protection. SOC-2, HIPAA, and GDPR compliance to safeguard your proprietary data</li>



<li class="advgb-dyn-271dfc27"><strong>Enterprise Reliability.</strong> Rely on secure, scalable hosting on our robust AWS infrastructure</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>



<div class="gs_item">
<h3 class="gs_title">
<button aria-controls="gs-content-gsbp-0a65b8e-1" id="gs-trigger-gsbp-0a65b8e-1" class="gs_click_sync" type="button" aria-expanded="false">
<span class="gs_name">02 Intuitive, Data-Driven, and Fast to Deploy</span>



<svg viewBox="0 0 896 1024" width="896" height="1024" class="gs_icon"><path d="M832 416h-288v-288c0-35.34-28.66-64-64-64h-64c-35.34 0-64 28.66-64 64v288h-288c-35.34 0-64 28.66-64 64v64c0 35.34 28.66 64 64 64h288v288c0 35.34 28.66 64 64 64h64c35.34 0 64-28.66 64-64v-288h288c35.34 0 64-28.66 64-64v-64c0-35.34-28.66-64-64-64z" /></svg>

</h3>



<div aria-labelledby="gs-trigger-gsbp-0a65b8e-1" id="gs-content-gsbp-0a65b8e-1" class="gs_content" role="region" aria-hidden="true">
<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-container gspb_container gspb_container-gsbp-88d5391" id="gspb_container-id-gsbp-88d5391">
<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-container gspb_container gspb_container-gsbp-ecdb547 why-atinary-content" id="gspb_container-id-gsbp-ecdb547">
<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-4cfb1bc" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-4cfb1bc ">02</div>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-ad828b0" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-ad828b0 ">Intuitive, Data-Driven, and Fast to Deploy</div>



<ul style="margin-right:0;margin-left:0" class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>User-Friendly No-Coding Interface. </strong>Navigate complex optimization with an intuitive UI that requires zero programming or AI expertise.</li>



<li><strong>Efficient Optimization</strong>. Solve multi-parameter and multi-objective problems with Bayesian Optimization (BO) with far fewer experiments and lower costs.</li>



<li><strong>Onboard in Under Two Hours.</strong> Deploy AI into your existing workflows.</li>



<li><strong>Actionable Analytics</strong>. Gain insights through built-in visualization and data modules for smarter, data-driven decisions.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>



<div class="gs_item">
<h3 class="gs_title">
<button aria-controls="gs-content-gsbp-0a65b8e-2" id="gs-trigger-gsbp-0a65b8e-2" class="gs_click_sync" type="button" aria-expanded="false">
<span class="gs_name">03 Built by Scientists and Trusted by Industry Leaders</span>



<svg viewBox="0 0 896 1024" width="896" height="1024" class="gs_icon"><path d="M832 416h-288v-288c0-35.34-28.66-64-64-64h-64c-35.34 0-64 28.66-64 64v288h-288c-35.34 0-64 28.66-64 64v64c0 35.34 28.66 64 64 64h288v288c0 35.34 28.66 64 64 64h64c35.34 0 64-28.66 64-64v-288h288c35.34 0 64-28.66 64-64v-64c0-35.34-28.66-64-64-64z" /></svg>
</button>
</h3>



<div aria-labelledby="gs-trigger-gsbp-0a65b8e-2" id="gs-content-gsbp-0a65b8e-2" class="gs_content" role="region" aria-hidden="true">
<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-container gspb_container gspb_container-gsbp-cd99345" id="gspb_container-id-gsbp-cd99345">
<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-container gspb_container gspb_container-gsbp-fa65196 why-atinary-content" id="gspb_container-id-gsbp-fa65196">
<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-5c2a1e0" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-5c2a1e0 ">03</div>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-9a16aca" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-9a16aca ">Built by Scientists and Trusted by Industry Leaders</div>



<ul style="margin-right:0;margin-left:0" class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>By Scientists, for Scientists.</strong> Developed by a multi-disciplinary team who understand real-world laboratory and scientific challenges.</li>



<li><strong>Validated by Experts. </strong>Trusted by pharmaceutical leaders, top-tier universities, and innovative labs worldwide.</li>



<li><strong>Diverse Applications.</strong> Proven across a wide range of life-, chemical-, and materials sciences sectors to drive research excellence.</li>



<li><strong>Accessible Innovation.</strong> Our no-coding approach ensures that any researcher can leverage AI without a background in coding, machine learning, or data science.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>



<div class="gs_item">
<h3 class="gs_title">
<button aria-controls="gs-content-gsbp-0a65b8e-3" id="gs-trigger-gsbp-0a65b8e-3" class="gs_click_sync" type="button" aria-expanded="false">
<span class="gs_name">04 Thought Leadership in AI-Driven R&amp;D</span>



<svg viewBox="0 0 896 1024" width="896" height="1024" class="gs_icon"><path d="M832 416h-288v-288c0-35.34-28.66-64-64-64h-64c-35.34 0-64 28.66-64 64v288h-288c-35.34 0-64 28.66-64 64v64c0 35.34 28.66 64 64 64h288v288c0 35.34 28.66 64 64 64h64c35.34 0 64-28.66 64-64v-288h288c35.34 0 64-28.66 64-64v-64c0-35.34-28.66-64-64-64z" /></svg>

<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-container gspb_container gspb_container-gsbp-5ed6858 why-atinary-content" id="gspb_container-id-gsbp-5ed6858">
<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-53cd116" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-53cd116 ">04</div>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-6e73f6d" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-6e73f6d ">Thought Leadership in AI-Driven R&amp;D</div>



<ul style="margin-right:0;margin-left:0" class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Pioneers of Self-Driving Labs®. </strong>We build intelligent solutions that dissolve traditional R&amp;D bottlenecks and allow scientists to focus on pure innovation.</li>



<li><strong>Accelerating Discovery and Development. </strong>We integrate AI and robotics with human expertise to achieve to achieve goals and breakthroughs in days or weeks, not years.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>



<div class="gs_item">
<h3 class="gs_title">

<svg viewBox="0 0 896 1024" width="896" height="1024" class="gs_icon"><path d="M832 416h-288v-288c0-35.34-28.66-64-64-64h-64c-35.34 0-64 28.66-64 64v288h-288c-35.34 0-64 28.66-64 64v64c0 35.34 28.66 64 64 64h288v288c0 35.34 28.66 64 64 64h64c35.34 0 64-28.66 64-64v-288h288c35.34 0 64-28.66 64-64v-64c0-35.34-28.66-64-64-64z" /></svg>

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-05769f7" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-05769f7 ">05</div>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-e7d44f7" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-e7d44f7 ">Seamless Integration and Connectivity</div>



<ul style="margin-right:0;margin-left:0" class="wp-block-list">
<li>Option to <strong>connect seamlessly</strong> with robotic hardware and data systems (LIMS, ELNs, etc.)</li>



<li><strong>Agnostic AI platform</strong> to enable Self-Driving Labs® mode</li>



<li>API and SDK access available</li>

</div>

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-b792f48" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-b792f48 "><strong>Formulation, Synthesis &amp; Catalysis Optimization</strong><br><br>Running DOE, OFAT, or iterative experiments in large parameter spaces? Atinary replaces slow human-driven trial-and-error with AI-driven R&amp;D, including process chemistry and small molecule synthesis.</div>
</div>
</div></div></div>



<div class="swiper-slide"><div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-swipe swiper-slide-inner gspb_sliderinner-id-gsbp-19fd422"><div class="slider-content-zone">

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-68308d6" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-68308d6 "><strong>Explore More, Experiment Less</strong><br><strong><br></strong>Efficiently screen large chemical spaces and find global optima with a fraction of experiments. Solve multi-parameter, multi-constraint, and multi-objective problems (yield, selectivity, cost, sustainability) faster than ever.</div>
</div>
</div></div></div>



<div class="swiper-slide"><div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-swipe swiper-slide-inner gspb_sliderinner-id-gsbp-e3d1531"><div class="slider-content-zone">

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-cfecbfc" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-cfecbfc "><strong>Reduce Development Time and Costs 5x to 100x</strong><br><strong><br></strong>Accelerate R&amp;D from years to months or weeks. Increase success rates, strengthen your product pipeline, and cut the cost of reaching global optima by more than 50%, compared to DOE and OFAT.</div>
</div>
</div></div></div>

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-1876c21" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-1876c21 "><strong>Digital R&amp;D Across Locations</strong><br><strong><br></strong>Working across multiple sites or teams? Atinary digitizes your R&amp;D workflows, enabling remote collaboration, knowledge sharing, and institutional knowledge retention. Hosted on our partners at AWS.</div>

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-3e6a9db" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-3e6a9db "><strong>Deploy AI in Under Two Hours</strong><br><strong><br></strong>All tech developed in house by chemists for chemists plus top team of engineers and developers. No coding or AI expertise required. Superior UI and UX, onboard in less than two hours, using only a browser.</div>

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-94c47f0" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-94c47f0 "><strong>Two days of work in 10 minutes</strong></div>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-7c36762" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-7c36762 ">“With Atinary SDLabs (+ robots and ACD Labs), I can set up my experiments in 5 minutes. This work used to take me 1 day with standard methods… In addition, visualizing and analyzing the results used to take me another day. Now I can do this in only 5 minutes with SDLabs’ data analytics module.”</div>
</div>



<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-container gspb_container gspb_container-gsbp-9f139b0" id="gspb_container-id-gsbp-9f139b0">
<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-12beeb1" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-12beeb1 ">Client at a large pharma company</div>
</div>
</div>
</div></div></div>

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-c7f88e2" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-c7f88e2 "><strong>From decades to weeks</strong></div>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-dc75837" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-dc75837 ">&#8220;… To accelerate catalyst development, we can now combine artificial intelligence and robotics, rationalizing the screening of chemical space and increasing the throughput and reproducibility of the experimental data. This groundbreaking approach achieves in weeks what traditionally took decades…”</div>
</div>



<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-container gspb_container gspb_container-gsbp-938efaf" id="gspb_container-id-gsbp-938efaf">
<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-bbbee09" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-bbbee09 "><a href="https://www.linkedin.com/in/paco-laveille/" target="_blank" rel="noopener" title="">Dr. Paco Laveille</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div></div></div>

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-f8d4f4d" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-f8d4f4d "><strong>Atinary SDLabs is like the iPhone of R&amp;D</strong></div>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-179e056" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-179e056 ">&#8220;We used the Atinary SDLabs® platform to guide our simulations on MIT’s supercomputer and to seamlessly integrate my lab equipment to deploy AI‑driven autonomous experimentation. Remarkably, within a week, my team completed tasks that I originally estimated would take more than two years! Its user friendliness and capabilities make SDLabs® the iPhone of R&amp;D.”</div>
</div>



<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-container gspb_container gspb_container-gsbp-dcb4204" id="gspb_container-id-gsbp-dcb4204">
<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-ba42815" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-ba42815 "><a href="https://dmse.mit.edu/people/faculty/alfredo-alexander-katz-2/" target="_blank" rel="noopener" title="">Dr. Alfredo Alexander‑Katz</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div></div></div>

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-9d0eaaa" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-9d0eaaa "><strong>Making the impossible possible</strong></div>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-608f11f" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-608f11f ">“From first meeting to results in a few weeks. We would not have been able to solve this complex optimization project without Atinary. The ROI was infinite for us!&#8221;</div>
</div>



<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-container gspb_container gspb_container-gsbp-21a43ad" id="gspb_container-id-gsbp-21a43ad">
<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-54ce689" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-54ce689 "><a href="https://www.linkedin.com/in/salomon-gilles-3a832b29/" target="_blank" rel="noopener" title="Gilles Salomon, Site Director, JAG Jakob SA"></a>Professor Buonassisi <br>Materials Science</div>
</div>
</div>
</div></div></div>



<div class="swiper-slide"><div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-swipe swiper-slide-inner gspb_sliderinner-id-gsbp-ef4e46c"><div class="slider-content-zone">

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-05a8b00" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-05a8b00 "><strong><strong>Reduced catalyst loading by 30x and the cost contribution</strong></strong></div>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-266cd73" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-266cd73 ">&#8220;We are pleased to announce that we are collaborating with Atinary Technologies on leveraging their proprietary AI‑driven platforms in Firmenich R&amp;D laboratories. Artificial intelligence, machine learning and the cutting‑edge digital technology behind self‑driving labs are providing new opportunities to speed and enhance our research and development. By combining Firmenich’s scientific expertise with Atinary’s Machine Learning algorithm, Falcon, and the SDLabs® software platform, we are accelerating process optimization towards industrialization of key ingredients.”</div>
</div>



<div class="wp-block-greenshift-blocks-container gspb_container gspb_container-gsbp-8b9a129" id="gspb_container-id-gsbp-8b9a129">
<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-385d7ca" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-385d7ca ">dsm‑firmenich</div>
</div>
</div>
</div></div></div>

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-f9d3a25" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-f9d3a25 "><strong><strong>Increased yields by 18% and reduced cost by 22%</strong></strong></div>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-140eb01" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-140eb01 ">“SDLabs allows simultaneous optimization of all variables and accelerated identification of global optima. In just 3 months, SDLabs led to significant improvements, with an 18% increase in product yield and a 22% reduction in cost, compared to a previously optimized process.&#8221;</div>
</div>

</div>
</div>
</div></div></div>

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-11a0117" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-11a0117 "><strong>Increased yields from 50% to 90% in three iterations</strong></div>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-797a3a1" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-797a3a1 ">&#8220;This partnership brings together our complementary expertise, paving the way for innovative AI solutions poised to transform pharmaceutical R&amp;D and accelerate drug discovery. Through this collaboration, Takeda will leverage Atinary’s leading AI Self‑Driving Labs technology, which, combined with its expertise in R&amp;D and drug discovery, aims to create a more robust and efficient drug development process, ultimately benefiting patients worldwide.”</div>
</div>

</div>
</div>
</div></div></div>

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-8ceaef7" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-8ceaef7 "><strong>Superior product and AI roadmap</strong></div>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-c4f91fb" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-c4f91fb ">&#8220;SDLabs significantly outperformed open-source packages and commercial alternatives we evaluated. It enabled us to efficiently explore a large and complex design space with remarkably few experiments. The technical support and AI roadmap are also impressive—clearly designed with the needs of researchers in mind.&#8221;</div>
</div>

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-3dbaefa" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-3dbaefa "><strong>Building the Labs of Tomorrow</strong></div>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-f53d2ed" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-f53d2ed ">“JAG Group and Atinary Technologies offer a unique turnkey solution that combines our expertise in robotics, automation, and artificial intelligence to transform R&amp;D and scientific discoveries with the Self‑Driving Labs® technology. We are laying the foundation today for the smart factories and the labs of the future.”</div>
</div>

</div>

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-15f7b79" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-15f7b79 "><strong>The Smart Way to Scale R&amp;D</strong></div>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-0338fd1" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-0338fd1 ">“We are very impressed with the Atinary team, technology and efficiency. Overall, this pilot project demonstrates that SDLabs® accelerates solar perovskite R&amp;D and process optimization. It reduces the time and costs to find optimal process parameters, and increases productivity, competitiveness and return on investment.”</div>
</div>

</div>
</div>

<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-6869f1d" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-6869f1d "><strong>We Would Not Have Found It Without Atinary</strong></div>



<div id="gspb_text-id-gsbp-a4afb1e" class="gspb_text gspb_text-id-gsbp-a4afb1e ">&#8220;Within three weeks of running our first campaign on Atinary&#8217;s AI platform, searching a high-dimensional reaction space, we identified a candidate reagent that was both higher-performing and greener than our reference. Interestingly, these conditions surprised our chemists &#8211; we would not have found it without Atinary.&#8221;</div>
</div>

</div>

<div class="gspb_id-gsbp-ec80a0c gspb-dynamic-post-image wp-block-greenshift-blocks-dynamic-post-image"><a href="https://atinary.com/use-cases/mit-no-code-bayesian-optimization-perovskite/" title="AI-Driven and Code-Free Bayesian Optimization for Perovskite Solar Cells"><img decoding="async" width="300" height="170" src="https://atinary.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/perovskite-solar-cell-manufacturing-300x170.webp" class=" wp-post-image" alt="An array of solar panels angled toward a bright, sunlit sky with scattered clouds at sunset." loading="lazy" srcset="https://atinary.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/perovskite-solar-cell-manufacturing-300x170.webp 300w, https://atinary.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/perovskite-solar-cell-manufacturing-1024x580.webp 1024w, https://atinary.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/perovskite-solar-cell-manufacturing-768x435.webp 768w, https://atinary.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/perovskite-solar-cell-manufacturing.webp 1200w" sizes="auto, (max-width: 300px) 100vw, 300px" /></a></div>

<div class="gspb_id-gsbp-4084066 gspb-dynamic-post-title wp-block-greenshift-blocks-dynamic-post-title"><h2 class="gspb-dynamic-title-element"><a href="https://atinary.com/use-cases/mit-no-code-bayesian-optimization-perovskite/">AI-Driven and Code-Free Bayesian Optimization for Perovskite Solar Cells</a></h2></div>

<div style="font-size:14px;padding-top:0;padding-bottom:0;margin-top:0;margin-bottom:0" class="wp-block-post-excerpt"><p class="wp-block-post-excerpt__excerpt">Atinary&#8217;s SDLabs AI platform enabled the Buonassisi Lab at MIT&hellip; </p></div></div>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
	document.querySelectorAll('ul li button').forEach(function (icon) {
		icon.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
		const submenu = this.closest('li')?.querySelector('ul.wp-block-navigation-submenu');
			if (submenu) {
				submenu.classList.toggle('submenu-active');
			}
		});
	})
});
</script><script data-wp-router-options="{&quot;loadOnClientNavigation&quot;:true}" fetchpriority="low" id="@wordpress/block-library/navigation/view-js-module" src="https://atinary.com/wp-includes/js/dist/script-modules/block-library/navigation/view.min.js?ver=1bf28ded04f9f188bdcb" type="module"></script>
<style class="wpcode-css-snippet">/* ============================================================
   Atinary Mobile Navigation
   All styles scoped to max-width: 991px — zero impact on desktop.
   ============================================================ */
@media (max-width: 991px) {

  /* ── Overlay backdrop ─────────────────────────────────────── */
  .atinary-nav-overlay {
    position: fixed !important;
    inset: 0 !important;
    z-index: 9998 !important;
    background: rgba(4, 8, 18, 0.72) !important;
    opacity: 0 !important;
    pointer-events: none !important;
    transition: opacity 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1) !important;
  }
  .atinary-nav-overlay.is-visible {
    opacity: 1 !important;
    pointer-events: all !important;
  }

  body.has-nav-open {
    overflow: hidden !important;
  }

  /* ── Hamburger button ─────────────────────────────────────── */
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-open {
    display: flex !important;
    flex-direction: column !important;
    align-items: center !important;
    justify-content: center !important;
    gap: 4px !important;
    width: 38px !important;
    height: 38px !important;
    padding: 8px !important;
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06) !important;
    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1) !important;
    border-radius: 5px !important;
    cursor: pointer !important;
    transition: background 0.2s, border-color 0.2s !important;
    position: relative !important;
    z-index: 10000 !important;
  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-open.is-open {
    padding-right: 16px !important;
  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-open:hover {
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1) !important;
    border-color: rgba(0, 200, 255, 0.35) !important;
  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-open svg path {
    transform-origin: center !important;
    transition: transform 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1), opacity 0.25s ease !important;
  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-open.is-open svg path:nth-child(1) {
    transform: translateY(3px) rotate(45deg) !important;
  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-open.is-open svg path:nth-child(2) {
    opacity: 0 !important;
    transform: scaleX(0) !important;
  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-open.is-open svg path:nth-child(3) {
    transform: translateY(-6px) rotate(-45deg) !important;
  }

  /* ── Drawer container ─────────────────────────────────────── */
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container {
    position: fixed !important;
    top: 80px !important;
    right: 0 !important;
    bottom: 0 !important;
    left: auto !important;
    width: 100% !important;
    height: 100dvh !important;
    z-index: 9999 !important;
    background: #080d14 !important;
    transform: translateX(110%) !important;
    transition: transform 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1) !important;
    display: flex !important;
    flex-direction: column !important;
    overflow: hidden !important;
    padding: 0 !important;
    margin: 0 !important;
    border: none !important;
    color: #e8edf5 !important;
  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container.is-menu-open {
    transform: translateX(0) !important;
  }

  /* ── Close wrapper row ────────────────────────────────────── */
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container
    .wp-block-navigation__responsive-close {
    flex-shrink: 0 !important;
    width: 100% !important;
    border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.07) !important;
    padding: 14px 20px !important;
    display: flex !important;
    align-items: center !important;
    justify-content: flex-end !important;
    background: transparent !important;
    position: static !important;
  }

  /* ── Close (X) button ─────────────────────────────────────── */
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-close {
    display: none !important;
  }

  /* ── Dialog wrapper ───────────────────────────────────────── */
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container
    .wp-block-navigation__responsive-dialog {
    flex: 1 !important;
    display: flex !important;
    flex-direction: column !important;
    overflow: hidden !important;
    position: static !important;
    width: 100% !important;
    margin: 0 !important;
    padding: 0 !important;
  }

  /* ── Scrollable content ───────────────────────────────────── */
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content {
    flex: 1 !important;
    overflow-y: auto !important;
    overflow-x: hidden !important;
    scrollbar-width: none !important;
    padding-bottom: 24px !important;
    position: static !important;
    width: 100% !important;
  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content::-webkit-scrollbar {
    display: none !important;
  }

  /* ── Top-level <ul>: force vertical stack ─────────────────── */
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    > .wp-block-navigation__container,
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    ul.wp-block-navigation__container {
    list-style: none !important;
    margin: 0 !important;
    padding: 0 !important;
    display: flex !important;
    flex-direction: column !important;
    align-items: stretch !important;
    justify-content: flex-start !important;
    gap: 0 !important;
    width: 100% !important;
    flex-wrap: nowrap !important;
  }

  /* ── Every <li> ───────────────────────────────────────────── */
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation-item {
    position: relative !important;
    display: block !important;
    width: 100% !important;
    border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06) !important;
    margin: 0 !important;
    padding: 0 !important;
    float: none !important;
  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation-item:last-child {
    border-bottom: none !important;
  }

  /* Accent bar on open item */
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation-item.is-open::before {
    content: '' !important;
    position: absolute !important;
    left: 0 !important;
    top: 0 !important;
    width: 2px !important;
    height: 50px !important;
    background: #00c8ff !important;
    border-radius: 0 2px 2px 0 !important;
    z-index: 1 !important;
  }

  /* ── Item anchor ──────────────────────────────────────────── */
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation-item__content {
    display: flex !important;
    align-items: center !important;
    padding: 0 50px 0 20px !important;
    height: 50px !important;
    font-size: 15px !important;
    font-weight: 500 !important;
    color: #e8edf5 !important;
    text-decoration: none !important;
    letter-spacing: 0.01em !important;
    transition: color 0.15s !important;
    width: 100% !important;
    box-sizing: border-box !important;
  }
  #gspb_container-id-gsbp-6ce054c  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation-item__content {
      color: #000000 !important;
    }
    #gspb_container-id-gsbp-6ce054c  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover {
      color: #000000 !important;
    }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover {
    color: #ffffff !important;
  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation-item.is-open
    > .wp-block-navigation-item__content {
    color: #00b9ed !important;
  }

  /* ── Chevron toggle button ────────────────────────────────── */
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation__submenu-icon {
    position: absolute !important;
    right: 0 !important;
    top: 0 !important;
    width: 50px !important;
    height: 50px !important;
    display: flex !important;
    align-items: center !important;
    justify-content: center !important;
    background: transparent !important;
    border: none !important;
    cursor: pointer !important;
    color: rgba(107, 122, 146, 0.8) !important;
    transition: color 0.2s !important;
    padding: 0 !important;
    margin: 0 !important;
  }
  
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation__submenu-icon:hover {
    color: #e8edf5 !important;
  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation__submenu-icon svg {
    transition: transform 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1) !important;
    stroke: currentColor !important;
    fill: none !important;
    width: 14px !important;
    height: 14px !important;
  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation-item.is-open
    > .wp-block-navigation__submenu-icon {
    color: #00c8ff !important;
  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation-item.is-open
    > .wp-block-navigation__submenu-icon svg {
    transform: rotate(180deg) !important;
  }

  /* ── Sub-menu containers — THE CORE FIX ──────────────────── */
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container {
    position: static !important;
    display: block !important;
    float: none !important;
    width: 100% !important;
    min-width: 0 !important;
    max-width: none !important;
    left: auto !important;
    right: auto !important;
    top: auto !important;
    bottom: auto !important;
    max-height: 0 !important;
    overflow: hidden !important;
    transition: max-height 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1) !important;
    background: #0e1520 !important;
    border: none !important;
    box-shadow: none !important;
    border-radius: 0 !important;
    opacity: 1 !important;
    visibility: visible !important;
    pointer-events: none !important;
    transform: none !important;
    z-index: auto !important;
    padding: 0 !important;
    margin: 0 !important;
    list-style: none !important;
  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container.is-open {
    max-height: 800px !important;
    pointer-events: all !important;
  }

  /* L2 items */
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container
    > .wp-block-navigation-item
    > .wp-block-navigation-item__content {
    padding-left: 36px !important;
    height: 44px !important;
    font-size: 14px !important;
    font-weight: 400 !important;
    color: rgba(232, 237, 245, 0.75) !important;
    position: relative !important;
  }
  #gspb_container-id-gsbp-6ce054c  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container
    > .wp-block-navigation-item
    > .wp-block-navigation-item__content {
      color: #000000 !important;
    }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container
    > .wp-block-navigation-item
    > .wp-block-navigation-item__content::before {
    content: '' !important;
    position: absolute !important;
    left: 22px !important;
    top: 50% !important;
    transform: translateY(-50%) !important;
    width: 4px !important;
    height: 4px !important;
    border-radius: 50% !important;
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2) !important;
    transition: background 0.2s !important;
  }
  #gspb_container-id-gsbp-6ce054c .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container
    > .wp-block-navigation-item
    > .wp-block-navigation-item__content::before {

  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container
    > .wp-block-navigation-item
    > .wp-block-navigation-item__content:hover::before,
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container
    > .wp-block-navigation-item.is-open
    > .wp-block-navigation-item__content::before {
    background: #00c8ff !important;
  }

  /* L2 toggle height */
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container
    > .wp-block-navigation-item
    > .wp-block-navigation__submenu-icon {

    width: 44px !important;
  }

  /* L3 container */
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container

  }

  /* L3 items */

    font-weight: 400 !important;

  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container
    .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container

    height: 3px !important;
  }
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content
    .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container

  }

  /* ── Contact Us CTA ───────────────────────────────────────── */
  .wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-content .wp-block-buttons {

    display: block !important;
    width: 100% !important;
    box-sizing: border-box !important;
  }
} /* end @media (max-width: 991px) */</style><script>/**
 * Atinary Mobile Navigation
 * Only activates on viewports ≤ 991px. Desktop is completely untouched.
 *
 * Menu closes ONLY via:
 *   - The hamburger button (toggle)
 *   - The Escape key
 *
 * Outside clicks are swallowed before WP's Interactivity API can act on them.
 * Inside clicks (sub-menus, links) are left to bubble normally.
 */
(function () {
  const mq = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 991px)');

  const openBtn   = document.querySelector('.wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-open');
  const closeBtn  = document.querySelector('.wp-block-navigation__responsive-container-close');
  const container = document.querySelector('.wp-block-navigation__responsive-container');
  const dialog    = document.querySelector('.wp-block-navigation__responsive-dialog');

  if (!openBtn || !container) return;

  // ── Strip WP Interactivity API bindings from the open button ──────────────
  if (mq.matches) {
    openBtn.removeAttribute('data-wp-on--click');
    openBtn.removeAttribute('data-wp-on--keydown');
  }

  // ── Open / close ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function openMenu() {
    container.classList.add('has-modal-open', 'is-menu-open');
    openBtn.classList.add('is-open');
    document.body.classList.add('has-nav-open');
    openBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
    const firstFocusable = dialog && dialog.querySelector('a, button, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])');
    if (firstFocusable) setTimeout(() => firstFocusable.focus(), 50);
  }

  function closeMenu() {
    container.classList.remove('has-modal-open', 'is-menu-open');
    openBtn.classList.remove('is-open');
    document.body.classList.remove('has-nav-open');
    openBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    container.querySelectorAll('.wp-block-navigation-item.is-open').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('is-open'));
    container.querySelectorAll('.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container.is-open').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('is-open'));
    container.querySelectorAll('.wp-block-navigation-submenu__toggle[aria-expanded="true"]').forEach(btn => btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'));
    openBtn.focus();
  }

  // ── Capture-phase guard ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Runs before WP's bubble-phase outside-click handler.
  // - Clicks OUTSIDE the drawer → stopImmediatePropagation so WP never sees
  //   them, and the menu stays open.
  // - Clicks INSIDE the drawer → let them bubble normally so sub-menu toggles
  //   and links work as expected.
  // - Clicks ON the hamburger → stopImmediatePropagation (we handle it below).
  document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    if (!mq.matches) return;
    if (!container.classList.contains('is-menu-open')) return;

    const insideDrawer  = container.contains(e.target);
    const isHamburger   = openBtn.contains(e.target);

    if (isHamburger) {
      // Hamburger while open → close. Stop WP seeing it.
      e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      closeMenu();
      return;
    }

    if (!insideDrawer) {
      // Outside click → swallow it, menu stays open.
      e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      return;
    }

    // Inside the drawer → do nothing here, let the event bubble to our
    // sub-menu toggle listeners below.
  }, true /* capture phase */);

  // ── MutationObserver — re-sync if WP strips classes anyway ───────────────
  new MutationObserver(() => {
    if (!mq.matches) return;
    if (!container.classList.contains('is-menu-open') && openBtn.classList.contains('is-open')) {
      openBtn.classList.remove('is-open');
      document.body.classList.remove('has-nav-open');
      openBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    }
  }).observe(container, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] });

  // ── Viewport resize ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  mq.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
    if (!e.matches) closeMenu();
  });

  function onlyMobile(fn) {
    return function (...args) {
      if (mq.matches) fn(...args);
    };
  }

  // ── Hamburger — handles opening (closing is handled in capture phase) ──────
  openBtn.addEventListener('click', onlyMobile(() => {
    if (!container.classList.contains('is-menu-open')) openMenu();
  }));

  // ── Close (X) button ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (closeBtn) {
    closeBtn.addEventListener('click', onlyMobile(closeMenu));
  }

  // ── Escape key ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (mq.matches && e.key === 'Escape' && container.classList.contains('is-menu-open')) {
      closeMenu();
    }
  });

  // ── Sub-menu toggles (L2 + L3) ────────────────────────────────────────────
  container.querySelectorAll('.wp-block-navigation-submenu__toggle').forEach(toggle => {
    toggle.addEventListener('click', onlyMobile((e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();

      const parentLi = toggle.closest('.wp-block-navigation-item');
      const subMenu  = parentLi && parentLi.querySelector(':scope > .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container');
      if (!parentLi || !subMenu) return;

      const isOpen = parentLi.classList.contains('is-open');

      // Accordion: close siblings at same level
      const siblings = parentLi.parentElement
        ? Array.from(parentLi.parentElement.children).filter(el => el !== parentLi)
        : [];
      siblings.forEach(sib => {
        sib.classList.remove('is-open');
        const sibSub    = sib.querySelector(':scope > .wp-block-navigation__submenu-container');
        const sibToggle = sib.querySelector(':scope > .wp-block-navigation-submenu__toggle');
        if (sibSub)    sibSub.classList.remove('is-open');
        if (sibToggle) sibToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
        sib.querySelectorAll('.wp-block-navigation-item.is-open').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('is-open'));
        sib.querySelectorAll('.wp-block-navigation__submenu-container.is-open').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('is-open'));
      });

      parentLi.classList.toggle('is-open', !isOpen);
      subMenu.classList.toggle('is-open', !isOpen);
      toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(!isOpen));
    }));
  });

  // ── Parent link → expand sub-menu instead of navigating ──────────────────
  container.querySelectorAll('.wp-block-navigation-item.has-child > .wp-block-navigation-item__content').forEach(link => {
    link.addEventListener('click', onlyMobile((e) => {
      const toggle = link.nextElementSibling;
      if (toggle && toggle.classList.contains('wp-block-navigation-submenu__toggle')) {
        e.preventDefault();
        toggle.click();
      }
    }));
  });

})();</script><script id="gsswiper-js" src="https://atinary.com/wp-content/plugins/greenshift-animation-and-page-builder-blocks/libs/swiper/swiper-bundle.min.js?ver=9.3.3"></script>
